**字段表**

| 字段 | 控件 | 必填 | 校验/说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 统计期间 | 日期范围 | 是 | 默认本年至今；最长 1 年 |
| 科室 | 下拉 | 否 | 默认本科室；质控科可选全部 |
| 汇总卡片 | 数字 | — | 方案总数/已审核/通过率/平均审核时长/驳回率 |
| 术式结构 | 横向条形图 | — | 按 `refractive_type` 分组计数 |
| RSB 裕度分布 | 表格+条图 | — | 5 个分级（NORMAL/CAUTION/WARN/CRITICAL/VIOLATION） |
| 平均/最小 RSB | 数字 | — | `AVG(min_rsb_um)`、`MIN(min_rsb_um)` |
| 晶体偏差分布 | 横向条形图 | — | 3 个状态占比 |
| 平均/最大偏差 | 数字 | — | `AVG(deviation_d)`、`MAX(abs(deviation_d))` |
| 风险分布 Top 8 | 表格 | — | 按 `risk_code` 分组计数 |
| 医师方案量 | 表格 | — | 方案数/通过/驳回/驳回率/平均 RSB |
| 驳回率异常提示 | 告警 | — | 驳回率 > 科室均值 2 倍时标 ⚠ |
| 月度趋势 | 柱状图 | — | 近 12 月方案量 |
| 导出 Excel | 按钮 | — | 含全部明细页签 |

**交互说明**

1. 点击任一图表可下钻到方案列表（如点「CRITICAL 28 例」→ P02 筛选裕度分级为 CRITICAL）。
2. **VIOLATION 恒为 0**：因为数据库 CHECK 阻止写入，报表展示 0 是「约束生效」的证明，页面标注说明。
3. **驳回率异常自动标记**：某医师驳回率 > 科室均值 2 倍时标 ⚠ 并给出质控建议。
4. **PENDING 偏差告警**：晶体计算存在 `PENDING` 时提示「有 1 例晶体计算未选择实际度数，M09 核对将失效」。
5. 全部报表支持导出，导出调 M02-06 审计。

**校验规则**

| # | 校验 | 错误码/表现 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 无 `m11:plan:stat` | 403 `"119001"` |
| 2 | 统计期间超 1 年 | `40001`，「统计期间不得超过 1 年」 |
| 3 | 日期起 > 止 | `40001` |
| 4 | 导出无权限 | `"119001"` |

---

## 6. 后端接口设计

### 6.1 接口总览

| 编号 | 方法 | 路径 | 说明 | 权限 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **工作台与统计** | | | | |
| API-01 | GET | `/api/v1/m11/plans/dashboard` | 方案工作台统计 | `m11:plan:view` |
| API-02 | GET | `/api/v1/m11/plans/statistics` | 方案统计报表 | `m11:plan:stat` |
| **方案列表与详情** | | | | |
| API-03 | GET | `/api/v1/m11/plans` | 方案分页列表 | `m11:plan:view` |
| API-04 | POST | `/api/v1/m11/plans` | 开立手术方案 | `m11:plan:create` |
| API-05 | GET | `/api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}` | 方案详情 | `m11:plan:view` |
| API-06 | PUT | `/api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}` | 修改方案基础信息 | `m11:plan:update` |
| API-07 | POST | `/api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/cancel` | 取消方案 | `m11:plan:delete` |
| API-08 | POST | `/api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/supersede` | 标记方案被替代 | `m11:plan:update` |
| **术前快照与术式** | | | | |
| API-09 | GET | `/api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/preop-snapshot` | 术前基线快照 | `m11:plan:view` |
| API-10 | POST | `/api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/preop-snapshot` | 重建术前快照 | `m11:plan:update` |
| API-11 | GET | `/api/v1/m11/plans/eligible-types` | 可选术式推荐（按患者条件） | `m11:plan:view` |
| **角膜切削计算** | | | | |
| API-12 | GET | `/api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/ablation` | 切削参数列表（按眼别） | `m11:plan:view` |
| API-13 | POST | `/api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/ablation/calculate` | 执行切削计算（不落库） | `m11:plan:update` |
| API-14 | POST | `/api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/ablation` | 确认切削参数并落库 | `m11:plan:update` |
| API-15 | GET | `/api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/ablation/calcs` | 切削计算历史留痕 | `m11:plan:view` |
| API-16 | POST | `/api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/ablation/{calc_id}/adopt` | 采纳某次计算结果 | `m11:plan:update` |
| **晶体度数计算** | | | | |
| API-17 | GET | `/api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/iol-calculation` | 晶体计算详情（含多公式明细） | `m11:plan:view` |
| API-18 | POST | `/api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/iol-calculation/calculate` | 执行晶体度数计算 | `m11:plan:update` |
| API-19 | POST | `/api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/iol-calculation/select` | 选择实际晶体度数 | `m11:plan:update` |
| API-20 | POST | `/api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/iol-calculation/confirm` | 确认晶体计算 | `m11:plan:update` |
| **风险提示** | | | | |
| API-21 | GET | `/api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/risks` | 风险提示清单 | `m11:plan:view` |
| API-22 | POST | `/api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/risks/acknowledge` | 风险确认与患者告知 | `m11:plan:update` |
| API-23 | POST | `/api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/risks/regenerate` | 重新生成风险提示 | `m11:plan:update` |
| **审核与签署** | | | | |
| API-24 | GET | `/api/v1/m11/plans/pending-audits` | 我的待审核队列 | `m11:plan:audit` |
| API-25 | POST | `/api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/submit` | 提交审核（生成版本快照） | `m11:plan:create` |
| API-26 | POST | `/api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/audit` | 审核通过 / 驳回 / 终审 | `m11:plan:audit` |
| API-27 | POST | `/api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/withdraw` | 撤回审核 | `m11:plan:create` |
| API-28 | POST | `/api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/consent` | 知情同意签署（固化快照） | `m11:plan:sign` |
| API-29 | GET | `/api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/audits` | 审核历史 | `m11:plan:view` |
| **版本与对比** | | | | |
| API-30 | GET | `/api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/versions` | 版本历史 | `m11:plan:view` |
| API-31 | GET | `/api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/versions/compare` | 版本差异对比 | `m11:plan:view` |
| **打印导出** | | | | |
| API-32 | GET | `/api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/print` | 方案打印数据 | `m11:plan:print` |
| API-33 | GET | `/api/v1/m11/plans/export` | 导出 Excel | `m11:plan:export` |
| **内部契约** | | | | |
| API-34 | GET | `/internal/v1/m11/iol-calculation/{patient_id}` | **P-01 晶体理论度数（M09-05 消费）** | 内部服务 |
| API-35 | GET | `/internal/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}` | **P-04 取手术方案（含切削参数）** | 内部服务 |
| API-36 | GET | `/internal/v1/m11/plans/by-patient/{patient_id}/latest` | **P-05 患者最近一次方案** | 内部服务 |
| API-37 | POST | `/internal/v1/m11/plans/on-audited` | **P-06 方案审核结果回调** | 内部服务 |

> **接口总数：37 个**（对外 33 个 + 内部契约 4 个），符合共享规范 §7.1 建议的 30~38 个。
> **四个内部契约路径核对（必须一字不差）：**
> - P-01 = API-34：`GET /internal/v1/m11/iol-calculation/{patient_id}`
> - P-04 = API-35：`GET /internal/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}`
> - P-05 = API-36：`GET /internal/v1/m11/plans/by-patient/{patient_id}/latest`
> - P-06 = API-37：`POST /internal/v1/m11/plans/on-audited`

### 6.2 统一响应格式与错误码

**统一响应格式**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {},
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**失败示例**

```json
{
  "code": "1104021",
  "message": "剩余基质床厚度 223.21μm 低于红线 250μm，禁止该方案",
  "data": {
    "rsb_um": 223.21,
    "redline_um": 250.00,
    "eye_side": "OD",
    "suggestions": [
      "缩小光学区至 6.00mm，预估 RSB 264.95μm",
      "改用 TransPRK（无瓣），预估 RSB 467.03μm",
      "改用 ICL 眼内镜手术"
    ]
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**分页格式**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "items": [],
    "total": 326,
    "page": 1,
    "page_size": 20,
    "pages": 17
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**本子模块错误码表（`"1104001"`~`"1104045"`）**

| 错误码 | 含义 | HTTP | 触发场景 | 用户提示文案 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `0` | 成功 | 200 | — | — |
| `40001` | 参数校验失败 | 400 | 通用入参错误 | 请检查输入内容 |
| `40100` | 未认证 | 401 | Token 失效 | 登录已过期，请重新登录 |
| `40300` | 无权限 | 403 | 缺权限编码 | 您没有该操作权限 |
| `40400` | 资源不存在 | 404 | 记录不存在 | 数据不存在或已删除 |
| `40900` | 版本冲突（乐观锁） | 409 | `version` 不匹配 | 数据已被他人修改，请刷新后重试 |
| `42200` | 业务规则校验失败 | 422 | 通用业务规则 | 业务校验未通过 |
| `42900` | 请求过于频繁 | 429 | 限流 | 操作过于频繁，请稍后重试 |
| `50000` | 服务器内部错误 | 500 | 未捕获异常 | 系统繁忙，请稍后重试 |
| `50300` | 服务不可用 | 503 | 依赖故障 | 服务暂时不可用，请稍后重试 |
| `"119001"` | M11 无此权限 | 403 | 模块级无权限 | 您没有 M11 屈光手术管理的操作权限 |
| `"1104001"` | 手术方案不存在或已作废 | 404 | `plan_no` 查无或已取消 | 手术方案不存在或已取消 |
| `"1104002"` | 方案已审核，不可修改 | 422 | 非草稿/驳回状态尝试编辑 | 方案已审核通过，不可修改；如需调整请开立新方案 |
| `"1104003"` | 方案取消原因不完整 | 422 | 取消原因 <10 字 | 取消原因必须不少于 10 字 |
| `"1104004"` | 当前状态不允许该操作 | 422 | 状态机前置状态不符 | 方案当前状态不允许该操作 |
| `"1104005"` | 方案已过期，需重新评估 | 422 | `CURRENT_DATE > expire_at` | 方案已过有效期（2027-01-29），请重新开立 |
| `"1104006"` | 方案已被新方案替代 | 422 | `status = 'SUPERSEDED'` | 该方案已被 FA202611010003 替代 |
| `"1104007"` | 患者不存在 | 422 | 调 M03 查无 | 患者信息不存在 |
| `"1104008"` | 术前检查单不存在或未完成 | 422 | 调 M11-02 查无或未完成 | 术前检查单不存在或尚未完成 |
| `"1104009"` | 术前准入结论非 PASS | 422 | `admission_result ≠ PASS` | 术前准入结论为 HOLD，不允许开立方案 |
| `"1104010"` | 角膜厚度不足 | 422 | CCT < 480μm（角膜激光类） | 中央角膜厚度 470.00μm 小于 480μm，角膜激光术式禁忌 |
| `"1104011"` | 术式与患者条件不匹配 | 422 | 屈光度/曲率/眼轴超术式范围 | SMILE 不适用球镜 -11.00D（范围 -10.00~+6.00D） |
| `"1104012"` | 所选术式不具备相应计算能力 | 422 | CXL/RK 调切削接口 | 术式 CXL 不执行角膜切削计算 |
| `"1104013"` | 眼内手术不执行切削计算 | 422 | `is_intraocular=TRUE` 调切削 | ICL 为眼内手术，请使用晶体度数计算 |
| `"1104014"` | 角膜激光手术不执行晶体计算 | 422 | 非眼内手术调晶体接口 | SMILE 为角膜激光手术，请使用切削参数计算 |
| `"1104015"` | 医师手术授权校验未通过 | 403 | M01-07 `auth/check` 失败 | 医师王×× 无 SMILE 手术授权 |
| `"1104016"` | 存在未确认的阻断级风险 | 422 | `acknowledged=FALSE` 的 FORBIDDEN 风险 | 存在 2 项阻断级风险未确认，不可提交审核 |
| `"1104017"` | 风险项已失效 | 422 | 参数变更后确认旧风险 | 该风险项已失效（参数已变更） |
| `"1104018"` | 术前基线快照缺失或不完整 | 422 | 快照未生成或关键字段为空 | 术前基线快照不完整，缺少中央角膜厚度 |
| `"1104019"` | 快照已冻结，不可修改 | 422 | 尝试修改已生成快照 | 术前快照已冻结，如需变更请取消方案重开 |
| `"1104020"` | 方案计算未完成，不可提交审核 | 422 | 切削/晶体计算未确认 | 切削参数尚未确认，不可提交审核 |
| `"1104021"` | 剩余基质床厚度低于红线 | 422 | RSB < 250μm | 剩余基质床厚度 223.21μm 低于红线 250μm，禁止该方案 |
| `"1104022"` | 切削计算输入缺失 | 422 | CCT/瓣帽厚度缺失 | 中央角膜厚度缺失，无法计算 RSB |
| `"1104023"` | 光学区或过渡区参数不合法 | 422 | OZ/TZ 越界或关系不符 | 过渡区宽度 0.60mm 不足 0.80mm |
| `"1104024"` | 切削计算失败或未收敛 | 422 | 算法异常/深度超限 | 最大切削深度 210.50μm 超出设备上限 200μm |
| `"1104025"` | 审核检查表未全部通过 | 422 | 存在 ❌ 项 | 检查表第 4 项（RSB 达标）未通过，不可通过 |
| `"1104026"` | 审核驳回信息不完整 | 422 | 驳回原因 <10 字或未选问题项 | 驳回必须填写原因（不少于 10 字）并选择问题项 |
| `"1104027"` | 知情同意未签署 | 422 | 终审时同意未签 | 知情同意未签署，不可终审通过 |
| `"1104028"` | 版本快照校验失败 | 422 | `snapshot_hash` 不匹配 | 版本快照校验失败，内容可能已被篡改 |
| `"1104029"` | 版本已存在，不可重复提交 | 409 | 同版本号重复 | 该版本已存在，请刷新后重试 |
| `"1104030"` | 晶体公式选择理由不完整 | 422 | `formula_reason` <10 字或 nomogram 缺编码 | 公式选择理由必须不少于 10 字 |
| `"1104031"` | 晶体计算输入超出合理范围 | 422 | AL/ACD/WTW/内皮越界 | 眼轴 37.20mm 超出 18.00~36.00mm 范围 |
| `"1104032"` | 前房深度或内皮不满足眼内手术条件 | 422 | ACD <2.80 或内皮 <2000 | 前房深度 2.65mm 不足 2.80mm，ICL 禁忌 |
| `"1104033"` | 角膜直径不满足晶体尺寸要求 | 422 | WTW <10.00 或 >13.50 | 角膜直径 9.50mm 超出 10.00~13.50mm |
| `"1104034"` | 晶体公式计算失败 | 422 | 公式库异常 | 晶体公式计算失败，请稍后重试 |
| `"1104035"` | 晶体度数偏差超阈值未说明 | 422 | `deviation` >0.25 且无理由 | 偏差 0.50D 超阈值 0.25D，必须填写说明 |
| `"1104036"` | 晶体计算未确认，不可用于核对 | 422 | 调 P-01 但未 CONFIRMED | 晶体计算尚未确认，请先确认 |
| `"1104037"` | 患者无当前有效晶体计算 | 404 | P-01 查无 `is_current` | 该患者暂无当前有效的晶体度数计算 |
| `"1104038"` | 方案审核时限已超 | 422 | `is_overdue=TRUE` 后操作 | 审核已超时 26 小时，仍可处理但已记录超时 |
| `"1104039"` | 内部契约入参不合法 | 422 | 内部接口参数缺失/格式错 | 内部契约调用参数不合法 |
| `"1104040"` | 上游服务不可用 | 503 | M01-07/M11-02/M15 不可用 | 上游服务暂时不可用，请稍后重试 |
| `"1104041"` | 医师权限不足以执行该术式终审 | 403 | 非科主任执行终审 | 仅科主任可执行终审 |
| `"1104042"` | 非方案提交人不可撤回 | 403 | 他人撤回 | 仅方案提交人可撤回审核 |
| `"1104043"` | 治疗方案重复开立 | 409 | 患者在管方案已存在 | 该患者已有在管方案 FA202610310002 |
| `"1104044"` | 风险确认人非方案医师 | 403 | 非主刀/设计医师确认 | 仅方案主刀或设计医师可确认风险 |
| `"1104045"` | 晶体型号库存不可及 | 422 | M15 查无可及 | 晶体型号 V4c -12.50D 暂无库存 |

> **错误码使用约束：** 本子模块共定义 45 个错误码，从 `"1104001"` 连续编号至 `"1104045"`，无断号、无空号；共享规范 §11 为 M11-04 分配的号段为 1104001 起至 1104999，本子模块仅使用其中前 45 个。模块通用码 `"119001"`（M11 无此权限）全模块复用，不重复定义。

### 6.3 接口详细设计

#### API-01 方案工作台统计

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m11/plans/dashboard?dept_id=201&doctor_id=3001&scope=mine
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "todo": {
      "pending_calc": 5,
      "pending_submit": 3,
      "pending_audit": 8,
      "rejected": 2,
      "expiring_soon": 4,
      "rsb_alert": 1
    },
    "rsb_violations": [
      {
        "plan_no": "FA202610310008",
        "patient_name": "赵××",
        "refractive_type": "SMILE",
        "min_rsb_um": 223.21,
        "redline_um": 250.00,
        "eye_side": "OU"
      }
    ],
    "my_plans": [],
    "type_distribution": [
      {"refractive_type": "SMILE", "count": 963, "ratio": 52.3},
      {"refractive_type": "FS_LASIK", "count": 494, "ratio": 26.8},
      {"refractive_type": "TRANSPRK", "count": 210, "ratio": 11.4},
      {"refractive_type": "ICL", "count": 149, "ratio": 8.1},
      {"refractive_type": "OTHER_REFRACTIVE", "count": 26, "ratio": 1.4}
    ],
    "rsb_margin_distribution": [
      {"grade": "NORMAL", "count": 1378, "ratio": 78.4},
      {"grade": "CAUTION", "count": 267, "ratio": 15.2},
      {"grade": "WARN", "count": 84, "ratio": 4.8},
      {"grade": "CRITICAL", "count": 28, "ratio": 1.6}
    ]
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 解析 `scope`（`mine` 只看本人、`dept` 看科室）与数据权限 2) 并发查询 6 个待办计数：待计算（`DRAFT` 且计算未确认）、待提交（`DRAFT` 且计算已确认）、待我审核（`m11_plan_audit.audit_result='PENDING'` 且 `audit_level` 匹配）、已驳回（`REJECTED`）、即将过期（`AUDITED` 且 `expire_at <= CURRENT_DATE + 7`）、RSB 告警（`rsb_redline_violated=TRUE` 或 `rsb_status='WARN'`）3) 查 RSB 违规清单（走 `idx_m11_plan_rsb`）4) 查本人在办方案前 10 条（走 `idx_m11_plan_patient_active` 的变体按 `chief_doctor_id`）5) 近 30 天术式分布与 RSB 裕度分布 6) 结果缓存 Redis 60 秒。

**错误码：** `40100`、`"119001"`

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验统计期间不超过 1 年 2) `summary` 聚合方案总数、已审核数、通过率、平均审核时长（`m11_plan_audit.duration_minutes`）、驳回率、平均/最小 RSB、RSB 违规数（恒为 0，因数据库约束）3) `type_distribution` 按 `refractive_type` 分组 4) `rsb_margin_distribution` 按 §3.4.8 的裕度分级用 CASE WHEN 分组 5) `iol_deviation_distribution` 按 `deviation_status` 分组 6) `risk_distribution` 用 `jsonb` 展开 `risk_code` 分组 7) `doctor_stats` 按 `chief_doctor_id` 分组，驳回率 > 科室均值 2 倍时置 `reject_rate_alert=true` 8) `monthly_trend` 按 `date_trunc('month', created_at)` 9) 结果缓存 300 秒。

**错误码：** `40001`（期间超 1 年）、`"119001"`

#### API-04 开立手术方案

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m11/plans
Idempotency-Key: 3f2a1b0c-9d8e-7f6a-5b4c-3d2e1f0a9b8c
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "patient_id": 10021,
  "preop_exam_no": "QJ202610310006",
  "record_no": "RG00001234",
  "refractive_type": "SMILE",
  "operation_code": "OP0023",
  "eye_side": "OU",
  "plan_source": "NEW",
  "previous_plan_no": null,
  "chief_doctor_id": 3001,
  "dept_id": 201,
  "remark": "患者希望术后次日恢复工作"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "id": 10241,
    "plan_no": null,
    "status": "DRAFT",
    "preop_snapshot_id": 8812,
    "auth_check_passed": true,
    "is_intraocular": false,
    "next_step": "CALCULATE_ABLATION"
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) `Idempotency-Key` 幂等（Redis `m11:idem:plan:create:{key}` TTL 24h）2) 调 M03-01 校验患者存在，取姓名/性别/年龄（`"1104007"`）3) 调 M11-02 取术前检查单，校验 `status='COMPLETED'` 且 `admission_result='PASS'`（`"1104008"`/`"1104009"`）4) 调 M01-07 `full-config` 取术式属性（`operation_level`、是否眼内手术、适用屈光度范围），校验患者屈光度在术式适用范围内（`"1104011"`）5) 调 M01-07 `auth/check` 校验主刀医师该术式授权（`"1104015"`），回执存 `auth_check_result` 6) 校验无重复在管方案（`"1104043"`）7) **自动生成术前基线快照**：从 M11-02 拉取全部术前数据写 `m11_plan_preop_snap`，存 `full_snapshot` + `snapshot_hash`；关键字段（CCT、AL、K1/K2）缺失则 `"1104018"` 8) 按 `refractive_type` 计算 `is_intraocular` 9) 写 `m11_plan`（`status='DRAFT'`，`plan_no=NULL`）10) 写日志 `CREATE_PLAN` + `SNAPSHOT_PREOP` 11) 返回 `next_step` 指示跳转页面。

**错误码：** `"1104007"`、`"1104008"`、`"1104009"`、`"1104010"`、`"1104011"`、`"1104015"`、`"1104018"`、`"1104043"`

```json
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 仅 `DRAFT` 或 `REJECTED` 状态可改，否则 `"1104002"` 2) `version` 乐观锁冲突 `40900` 3) 更换主刀医师时重新调 `auth/check`（`"1104015"`）4) 改眼别时若已有切削参数，需清除并提示重新计算 5) 写日志 `UPDATE_PLAN` + `changed_fields`。

**错误码：** `"1104002"`、`40900`、`"1104015"`

**业务逻辑：** 1) 仅非终态可取消（`EXPIRED`/`CANCELLED`/`SUPERSEDED` 拒绝，`"1104004"`）2) `cancel_reason` ≥10 字，否则 `"1104003"` 3) 置 `status='CANCELLED'`、`cancel_reason`、`cancelled_at` 4) 若已审核，发布事件 `m11.plan.cancelled` 通知 M06/M11-05 5) 写日志 `CANCEL_PLAN` + snapshot。

**错误码：** `"1104001"`、`"1104004"`

#### API-13 执行切削计算（不落库）

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/ablation/calculate
Content-Type: application/json

  "optical_zone_type": "ASPIRATION",
  "nomogram_adjust_d": null,
  "oz_note": null,
  "target_sphere": 0.00
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验方案状态 ∈ {`DRAFT`,`REJECTED`}，否则 `"1104002"` 2) 校验方案非眼内手术 `"1104013"` 3) 校验 OZ ∈ 5.50~8.00（`"1104023"`）、TZ ≥ OZ 且 ≤9.50（`"1104023"`）、TZ 单侧宽度 ≥0.80（`"1104023"`）4) 校验厚度基准可用：CCT 非空（`"1104022"`）；SMILE 帽厚必填、FS-LASIK 瓣厚必填（`"1104022"`）5) 按 §3.4.1 计算球镜分量与柱镜分量深度、最大切削深度、组织切削量 6) 按 §3.4.3 计算 RSB 与裕度（厚度基准取 `min(CCT, thinnest)`）7) **校验 RSB ≥ 250μm**：若低于则返回 `"1104021"` 并在 `data.suggestions` 给出优化方案 8) 校验切削深度 ≤200μm（`"1104024"`）9) 生成 `calc_no`、`calc_seq`（本方案本眼 +1）10) **写 `m11_plan_ablation_calc` 留痕**（`is_adopted=FALSE`）11) 返回完整计算过程（含 `formula_display` 供页面展示公式代入过程）。

**错误码：** `"1104002"`、`"1104013"`、`"1104021"`、`"1104022"`、`"1104023"`、`"1104024"`

**响应**

```json
  "data": {
    "id": 7701,
    "eye_side": "OD",
    "rsb_um": 347.03,
    "rsb_status": "PASS",
    "feasible": true,
    "plan_min_rsb_um": 347.03,

  },

}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验方案状态 `"1104002"` 2) 校验 `calc_id` 对应计算记录存在且 `feasible=TRUE`，否则 `"1104024"` 3) **服务端重算一遍**（不采信前端传值），若与留痕不一致则 `"1104024"` 4) **再次校验 RSB ≥ 250μm**（`"1104021"`）5) UPSERT `m11_plan_ablation`（`uk_m11_plan_ablation_eye`）6) 把对应 `m11_plan_ablation_calc` 置 `is_adopted=TRUE`、`adopted_at`（部分唯一索引保证唯一）7) 重新计算方案最小 RSB `min_rsb_um`（双眼取小）、`ablation_feasible` 写回 `m11_plan` 8) 触发风险重算（异步）9) 写日志 `CONFIRM_ABLATION` + snapshot 10) 返回双眼是否都已确认。

**错误码：** `"1104002"`、`"1104021"`、`"1104024"`

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验方案为眼内手术，否则 `"1104014"` 2) **校验 `formula_reason` ≥10 字**，否则 `"1104030"` 3) `formula_code='VENDOR_NOMOGRAM'` 时 `vendor_nomogram_code` 必填（`"1104030"`）4) 从术前快照取 AL、K1/K2、ACD、LT、WTW、球镜；校验合理范围（`"1104031"`）5) **眼内手术安全校验**：ACD ≥2.80（`"1104032"`）、WTW 10.00~13.50（`"1104033"`）、内皮 ≥2000（`"1104032"`）、AL 18.00~36.00（`"1104031"`）6) 调 `IOLFormulaEngine` 计算主用公式（`"1104034"` 失败）7) 并行计算交叉验证公式 8) 计算极差与离群标记（`>0.75D` 为离群）9) 按厂商 nomogram 计算晶体尺寸与预估拱高 10) 调 M15 查库存可及性（失败降级 `material_available=null`）11) **写 `m11_iol_calc` + `m11_iol_calc_detail`**（新记录 `is_current=TRUE`，原记录置 `FALSE`+`SUPERSEDED`）12) 写日志 `CALC_IOL`。

**错误码：** `"1104014"`、`"1104030"`、`"1104031"`、`"1104032"`、`"1104033"`、`"1104034"`、`"1104045"`

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "calc_no": "IOL202610310003",
    "theoretical_power_d": -12.50,
    "selected_power_d": -12.50,
    "deviation_d": 0.00,

    "deviation_tolerance_d": 0.25
  },
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验计算记录存在且 `is_current=TRUE`（`"1104037"`）2) 按 §3.4.6 计算偏差 `deviation = selected − theoretical` 3) 判定 `deviation_status`：`abs ≤ tolerance` → `WITHIN_TOLERANCE`；`> tolerance` → `EXCEED` 4) `EXCEED` 且 `deviation_reason` ≥10 字 → `JUSTIFIED`；`EXCEED` 且无理由 → `"1104035"` 5) 校验选择度数在 −30~+40D（`"1104031"`）6) 环曲面晶体必须给轴位 7) 写 `selected_*`、`deviation_*`、`iol_size_mm` 8) 写日志 `SELECT_IOL_POWER`。

**错误码：** `"1104031"`、`"1104035"`、`"1104037"`

```json
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验操作人为方案主刀或设计医师，否则 `"1104044"` 2) 逐条校验 `risk_id` 属于该方案且 `is_active=TRUE`，失效项拒绝 `"1104017"` 3) 写 `acknowledged=TRUE`、`acknowledged_by`、`acknowledged_at`、`ack_remark` 4) `patient_notified=true` 时写 `patient_notified=TRUE`、`patient_notified_at` 5) 计算剩余未确认的阻断级风险数 6) 写日志 `ACK_RISK` / `NOTIFY_PATIENT` 7) 返回 `ready_to_submit`（阻断级全确认且已告知才为 TRUE）。

**错误码：** `"1104017"`、`"1104044"`、`40001`

```
POST /api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/risks/regenerate
Content-Type: application/json

{"regenerate_reason": "切削参数已调整，需重新评估风险项"}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "deactivated_count": 3,
    "generated_count": 2,
    "resolved_count": 1,
    "items": [
      {"risk_code": "OZ_SMALLER_THAN_PUPIL", "risk_level": "MEDIUM", "is_new": true},
      {"risk_code": "DRY_EYE", "risk_level": "MEDIUM", "is_new": true}
    ]
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验方案状态 ∈ {`DRAFT`,`REJECTED`}，否则 `"1104002"` 2) 把现有活跃风险置 `is_active=FALSE` 3) 运行风险引擎，按当前切削/晶体参数重新匹配 20 项风险编码 4) 新命中的写入（`is_active=TRUE`）；对之前命中但现已不满足的，若判定为「参数调整后消除」则置 `resolved=TRUE` + `resolved_by_change` 5) 写日志 `GENERATE_RISK` 6) 返回统计。

**错误码：** `"1104002"`

#### API-25 提交审核

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/submit
Idempotency-Key: 7a6b5c4d-3e2f-1a0b-9c8d-7e6f5a4b3c2d
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "required_levels": 3,
  "submit_note": "方案已完成切削计算与风险确认，提交审核",
  "version": 5
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "plan_no": "FA202610310002",
    "status": "PENDING_AUDIT",
    "version_no": 3,
    "audit_id": 33001,
    "audit_level": 2,
    "audit_deadline": "2026-11-01T10:20:00",
    "snapshot_hash": "b4e3d2c1a0f9e8d7c6b5a4f3e2d1c0b9a8f7e6d5c4b3a2f1e0d9c8b7a6f5e4d3"
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) `Idempotency-Key` 幂等 2) 校验状态 ∈ {`DRAFT`,`REJECTED`}，否则 `"1104004"` 3) **校验计算已完成**：角膜激光类需两眼 `m11_plan_ablation` 均已确认；眼内镜类需 `m11_iol_calc.status='CONFIRMED'`；否则 `"1104020"` 4) **校验授权有效**：重新调 M01-07 `auth/check`（`"1104015"`）5) **校验阻断级风险已确认且已告知**，否则 `"1104016"` 6) **重新调 M01-07 `auth/check` 并写回 `auth_check_result`** 7) **生成版本快照**：把方案 + 术前快照 + 切削/晶体 + 风险汇总成 `snapshot_json`，计算 `snapshot_hash`，写 `m11_plan_version`（`version_no = current_version + 1`、`previous_version_no`、`change_reason`（非首版必填 ≥10 字））8) 首版提交时分配 `plan_no`（Redis `INCR m11:code:FA:{yyyyMMdd}`）9) 置 `status='PENDING_AUDIT'`、`submitted_at`、`current_version+1` 10) 创建 `m11_plan_audit`（`audit_action='SUBMIT'`、`audit_result='PENDING'`、`audit_level=2`、`required_levels`、`audit_deadline`）11) 写日志 `SUBMIT_AUDIT` + `CREATE_VERSION` 12) 返回审核信息。

**错误码：** `"1104004"`、`"1104015"`、`"1104016"`、`"1104020"`、`"1104029"`

#### API-26 审核（通过 / 驳回 / 终审）

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}/audit
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "audit_action": "APPROVE",
  "audit_result": "PASSED",
  "audit_opinion": "方案合理，RSB 裕度 97μm 属 CAUTION 级，OZ 6.50mm 与暗瞳匹配良好，同意通过。",
  "reject_reason": null,
  "reject_items": null,
  "checklist_result": null,
  "version": 6
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "plan_no": "FA202610310002",
    "status": "AUDITED",
    "audit_id": 33002,
    "audit_level": 3,
    "audit_result": "PASSED",
    "audited_at": "2026-10-31T14:30:00",
    "expire_at": "2027-01-29",
    "auth_rechecked": true,
    "auth_check_passed": true
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验当前用户有权在该 `audit_level` 审核（`"1104041"`，终审仅科主任）2) 校验方案 `status='PENDING_AUDIT'`（`"1104004"`）3) **强制重做授权校验**：调 M01-07 `auth/check`，失败则 `"1104015"` 且不允许通过 4) **自动生成审核检查表 11 项**（§B.5），审核人不可主观勾选；有 ❌ 项时不允许 `PASSED`（`"1104025"`）5) **驳回分支**：`audit_action='REJECT'` 时必须 `audit_result='REJECTED'` + `reject_reason` ≥10 字 + `reject_items` 非空，否则 `"1104026"`；置方案 `status='REJECTED'`、`reject_reason` 6) **通过分支**：判断是否达终级（`audit_level == required_levels`）：
   - 非终级 → 方案保持 `PENDING_AUDIT`，创建下一级 `m11_plan_audit`（`audit_level+1`）
   - 终级 → **校验知情同意已签**（否则 `"1104027"`）→ 置 `status='AUDITED'`、`audited_at`、`expire_at = audited_at + 90 天`（§3.4.7）→ 发布事件 `m11.plan.audited` → **回调 M06 P-06**
   7) 写 `m11_plan_audit`（含 `checklist_result`、`checklist_passed_count/total_count`、`auth_rechecked`、`auth_check_result`、`duration_minutes`、`from_status`/`to_status`）8) 写日志 `AUDIT_PASS` / `AUDIT_REJECT` / `APPROVE_PLAN` + snapshot。

**错误码：** `"1104004"`、`"1104015"`、`"1104025"`、`"1104026"`、`"1104027"`、`"1104041"`

```json
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验方案 `status='PENDING_AUDIT'`（`"1104004"`）2) 校验操作人为提交人本人（`"1104042"`）3) 校验尚无已完成的审核动作（若已有一级通过则不可撤回）4) `withdraw_reason` ≥10 字 5) 置 `status='DRAFT'`，作废待审的 `m11_plan_audit` 记录 6) 写日志 `WITHDRAW_AUDIT`。

**错误码：** `"1104004"`、`"1104042"`、`40001`

```json
    "is_sealed": true,
    "sealed_at": "2026-10-31T14:30:05",
    "snapshot_hash": "c5f4e3d2c1b0a9f8e7d6c5b4a3f2e1d0c9b8a7f6e5d4c3b2a1f0e9d8c7b6a5f4",
    "pdf_file_id": 99120,
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验方案 `status='PENDING_AUDIT'`（`"1104004"`）2) 校验非本人签署必须有见证人（`40001`）3) 校验告知医师为方案主刀或设计医师 4) 校验 `inform_duration_minutes` 合理（1~240）；<5 分钟时写 `warn` 但允许 5) **生成方案快照**：取当前方案 + 术前快照 + 切削/晶体 + **当前活跃风险编码数组** 6) 计算 `snapshot_hash`（SHA-256）7) 生成 PDF 存文件服务 8) 写 `m11_plan_consent_snap`（`is_sealed=TRUE`、`sealed_at`、`risk_codes_snapshot`、`risk_count`、`qa_record`）9) 回写 `m11_plan.consent_signed=TRUE` 10) 写日志 `SIGN_CONSENT` + snapshot 11) **固化后不可修改**（数据库 `ck_m11_plan_consent_immutable` 兜底）。

**错误码：** `"1104004"`、`40001`

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验两版本均属于该方案（`40001`）2) 解析各自 `snapshot_json` 3) 逐字段对比（11 个关键参数），计算 `delta` 4) 自动生成 `impact` 影响说明（OZ 降 → 切削降 → 组织更节约；RSB 升 → 更安全）5) 若 `from_version` 被驳回，附驳回追溯信息（`m11_plan_audit`）6) 附 `to_version` 的修订原因 7) 校验 `snapshot_hash`，不匹配 `"1104028"`。

**错误码：** `"1104001"`、`"1104028"`、`40001`

#### API-33 导出 Excel

```
GET /api/v1/m11/plans/export
    ?start_date=2026-01-01&end_date=2026-10-31&dept_id=201&export_type=PLAN
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "task_id": "exp_20261031_m11_04_0001",
    "export_type": "PLAN",
    "status": "PENDING",
    "estimated_seconds": 12,
    "max_rows": 50000
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `m11:plan:export` 权限 2) `export_type ∈ {PLAN, ABLATION, IOL, RISK, AUDIT, ALL}` 3) 创建异步导出任务（Celery `m11.plan.export`）4) 单次上限 50,000 行，超出提示缩小日期范围 5) 写日志 `EXPORT` 并调 M02-06 审计（含患者信息）。

**错误码：** `40001`、`"119001"`

#### API-34 晶体理论度数（P-01 内部契约）★

**请求**

```
GET /internal/v1/m11/iol-calculation/{patient_id}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "found": true,
    "calc_no": "IOL202610310003",
    "patient_id": 10031,
    "refractive_type": "ICL",
    "eye_side": "OD",
    "formula_code": "SRK_T",
    "axial_length_mm": 25.40,
    "anterior_chamber_depth_mm": 3.20,
    "white_to_white_mm": 12.00,
    "theoretical_power_d": -12.50,
    "selected_power_d": -12.50,
    "deviation_d": 0.00,
    "deviation_status": "WITHIN_TOLERANCE",
    "deviation_tolerance_d": 0.25,
    "iol_model": "V4c",
    "iol_size_mm": 13.70,
    "status": "CONFIRMED",
    "confirmed_at": "2026-10-31T11:15:00",
    "plan_no": "FA202610300011"
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 按 `patient_id` 查 `is_current=TRUE` 且 `is_deleted=FALSE` 的记录，**走 `uk_m11_iol_calc_current` 唯一索引，单行查找（P95 ≤ 200ms 有保障）** 2) 无记录时返回 `found=false` 且 `data` 中仅含 `patient_id`，**HTTP 200 而非 404**（M09 降级路径需要能区分「无计算」与「服务不可用」）3) 返回 `theoretical_power_d`（理论度数）、`selected_power_d`（选择度数）、`deviation_d`、`deviation_tolerance_d` 四项核对必需字段 4) **只读，不产生任何写入** 5) 结果缓存 Redis 120 秒（`m11:plan:iol:{patient_id}`），晶体计算确认/变更时失效 6) 记录调用日志（`action='QUERY_IOL_POWER'`，`operator_role='SYSTEM'`）。

**调用方：** **M09-05（U-04 已预留，最关键）**、M06（排台核对）。

**M09 使用约定：** M09 拿 `theoretical_power_d` 与术中实际植入度数比对，偏差 ≤ `deviation_tolerance_d`（0.25D）视为通过；若本接口不可用，M09 降级为「跳过比对，标记未校验」。

**错误码：** `"1104039"`（patient_id 非法）、`"1104040"`（服务不可用）

#### API-35 取手术方案（P-04 内部契约）

**请求**

```
GET /internal/v1/m11/plans/{plan_no}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "plan_no": "FA202610310002",

    "patient_name": "张××",
    "age": 26,
    "refractive_type": "SMILE",
    "operation_code": "OP0023",

    "eye_side": "OU",
    "is_intraocular": false,
    "status": "AUDITED",
    "current_version": 3,
    "chief_doctor_id": 3001,
    "chief_doctor_name": "王××",
    "auth_check_passed": true,
    "audited_at": "2026-10-31T14:30:00",
    "expire_at": "2027-01-29",
    "min_rsb_um": 347.03,
    "rsb_redline_violated": false,
    "ablation_feasible": true,
    "ablations": [
      {
        "eye_side": "OD",
        "oz_mm": 6.50,
        "tz_mm": 8.10,
        "ablation_max_um": 64.97,
        "flap_thickness_um": null,
        "cap_thickness_um": 120.00,
        "rsb_um": 347.03,
        "rsb_status": "PASS",
        "algo_code": "MUNNERLYN",
        "algo_version": "2.1.0"
      },
      {
        "eye_side": "OS",
        "oz_mm": 6.50,
        "tz_mm": 8.10,
        "ablation_max_um": 62.14,
        "flap_thickness_um": null,
        "cap_thickness_um": 120.00,
        "rsb_um": 347.86,
        "rsb_status": "PASS",
        "algo_code": "MUNNERLYN",
        "algo_version": "2.1.0"
      }
    ],

    "consent_signed": true,

  },

```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 按 `plan_no` 查主档，不存在 `"1104001"` 2) 关联查 `m11_plan_ablation`（按眼别）与 `m11_iol_calc`（眼内手术时）3) 组装扁平结构（不返回从表全量，只返回执行侧需要的字段）4) **返回 `status` 供 M06 校验**：仅 `AUDITED` 允许排台 5) **返回 `expire_at` 供 M06 校验时效**：`CURRENT_DATE > expire_at` 时应拒绝排台 6) 只读，SLA P95 ≤ 150ms 7) 结果缓存 Redis 120 秒，方案状态变更时失效。

**调用方：** M06（排台前校验）、M09（术中核对切削参数）、M16-04（收费项推导）。

**错误码：** `"1104001"`、`"1104005"`（已过期）、`"1104040"`

#### API-36 患者最近一次方案（P-05 内部契约）

**请求**

```
GET /internal/v1/m11/plans/by-patient/{patient_id}/latest
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "found": true,
    "plan_no": "FA202610310002",
    "refractive_type": "SMILE",
    "eye_side": "OU",
    "status": "AUDITED",
    "current_version": 3,
    "audited_at": "2026-10-31T14:30:00",
    "expire_at": "2027-01-29",
    "min_rsb_um": 347.03,
    "rsb_redline_violated": false,
    "risk_level": "MEDIUM",
    "chief_doctor_name": "王××",
    "is_intraocular": false
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2WY"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 按 `patient_id` 查 `status IN ('DRAFT','PENDING_AUDIT','AUDITED')` 的记录（走 `idx_m11_plan_patient_active`）2) 排序优先级：`AUDITED` > `PENDING_AUDIT` > `DRAFT`，同级按 `created_at DESC` 3) 取第一条 4) 无记录返回 `found=false`（HTTP 200）5) 只读，SLA P95 ≤ 120ms 6) 结果缓存 Redis 120 秒。

**调用方：** M06、M09、M07（医生工作站查看患者屈光方案）。

**错误码：** `"1104039"`、`"1104040"`

#### API-37 方案审核结果回调（P-06 内部契约）

**请求**

```
POST /internal/v1/m11/plans/on-audited
Idempotency-Key: 9b8a7c6d-5e4f-3a2b-1c0d-9e8f7a6b5c4d
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "plan_no": "FA202610310002",
  "audit_result": "PASSED",
  "audited_at": "2026-10-31T14:30:00",
  "auditor_id": 3009,
  "auditor_name": "刘主任",
  "caller": "M06",
  "caller_ref": "APPLY-20261031008"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "plan_no": "FA202610310002",
    "status": "AUDITED",
    "can_schedule": true,
    "expire_at": "2027-01-29",
    "synced": true
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) `Idempotency-Key` 幂等（同一 `plan_no + caller_ref` 只处理一次）2) 按 `plan_no` 查找方案，不存在 `"1104001"` 3) **本接口是 M06 排台前的确认回调**：M06 传入自己的审核判定结果，M11-04 返回自己侧的方案状态与是否可排台 4) 校验 `audit_result` 与本地状态一致性：
   - M06 判定 `PASSED` 但本地非 `AUDITED` → 返回 `can_schedule=false` 并说明本地状态
   - 一致 → 返回 `can_schedule=true` 5) **不修改方案状态**（状态由本模块审核流唯一维护；本接口只读校验 + 记录回执）6) 记录调用日志（`action='AUTH_RECHECK'` 附 `caller`、`caller_ref`）7) 只读语义，SLA P95 ≤ 100ms。

> **硬规则：** 本接口**不改变** `m11_plan.status`。M06 不能通过回调把方案改成已审核——状态只能由 API-26 的审核流产生。

**调用方：** M06（排台前校验）。
**错误码：** `"1104001"`、`"1104039"`、`"1104040"`

### 6.4 FastAPI 路由实现示例

```python
# app/api/v1/m11/plans.py
"""M11-04 手术方案与切削计算 —— 路由层"""

from datetime import date
from typing import Literal, Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Header, Query, status
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.api.deps import get_current_user, get_db, require_perm
from app.core.response import ApiResponse
from app.schemas.m11.plan import (
    AblationCalcIn,
    ConsentSignIn,
    FittingQueryIn,
    IolCalcIn,
    IolSelectIn,
    PlanAuditIn,
    PlanCreateIn,
    PlanDetailOut,
    PlanListOut,
    PlanStatisticsOut,
    PlanUpdateIn,
    RiskAckIn,
)
from app.services.m11.plan_service import PlanService
from app.services.m11.ablation_service import AblationService
from app.services.m11.iol_service import IolService

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/m11/plans", tags=["M11-04 手术方案与切削计算"])


def get_plan_service(db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)) -> PlanService:
    """构造方案服务。"""
    return PlanService(db)


def get_ablation_service(db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)) -> AblationService:
    """构造切削计算服务。"""
    return AblationService(db)


def get_iol_service(db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)) -> IolService:
    """构造晶体计算服务。"""
    return IolService(db)


# ---------- 工作台与统计 ----------

@router.get(
    "/dashboard",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="方案工作台统计",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m11:plan:view"))],
)
async def get_dashboard(
    dept_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, gt=0),
    doctor_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, gt=0),
    scope: Literal["mine", "dept"] = Query("mine"),
    svc: PlanService = Depends(get_plan_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """返回 6 个待办计数、RSB 违规清单、在办方案、术式与裕度分布。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.get_dashboard(dept_id, doctor_id, scope))


@router.get(
    "/statistics",
    response_model=ApiResponse[PlanStatisticsOut],
    summary="方案统计报表",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m11:plan:stat"))],
)
async def get_statistics(
    start_date: date = Query(..., description="统计起始日"),
    end_date: date = Query(..., description="统计截止日"),
    dept_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, gt=0),
    svc: PlanService = Depends(get_plan_service),
) -> ApiResponse[PlanStatisticsOut]:
    """术式结构、RSB 裕度分布、晶体偏差分布、风险分布、医师方案量。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.get_statistics(start_date, end_date, dept_id))


# ---------- 方案主档 ----------

@router.get(
    "",
    response_model=ApiResponse[PlanListOut],
    summary="方案分页列表",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m11:plan:view"))],
)
async def list_plans(
    keyword: Optional[str] = Query(None, max_length=64),
    status_: Optional[str] = Query(None, alias="status"),
    refractive_type: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    risk_level: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    start_date: Optional[date] = Query(None),
    end_date: Optional[date] = Query(None),
    rsb_violated_only: bool = Query(False),
    pending_audit_only: bool = Query(False),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=200),
    svc: PlanService = Depends(get_plan_service),
) -> ApiResponse[PlanListOut]:
    """按关键词、术式、状态、风险、日期筛选方案列表。"""
    q = FittingQueryIn(
        keyword=keyword,
        status=status_,
        refractive_type=refractive_type,
        risk_level=risk_level,
        start_date=start_date,
        end_date=end_date,
        rsb_violated_only=rsb_violated_only,
        pending_audit_only=pending_audit_only,
        page=page,
        page_size=page_size,
    )
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.list_plans(q))


@router.post(
    "",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
    summary="开立手术方案",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m11:plan:create"))],
)
async def create_plan(
    body: PlanCreateIn,
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: PlanService = Depends(get_plan_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """开立方案：校验准入、术式适用范围、医师授权，并自动生成术前基线快照。"""
    result = await svc.create_plan(body, operator=user, idem_key=idempotency_key)
    return ApiResponse.ok(result)


@router.get(
    "/{plan_no}",
    response_model=ApiResponse[PlanDetailOut],
    summary="方案详情",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m11:plan:view"))],
)
async def get_plan(
    plan_no: str,
    svc: PlanService = Depends(get_plan_service),
) -> ApiResponse[PlanDetailOut]:
    """返回方案主档与术前快照、切削参数、晶体计算、风险、审核历史。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.get_plan_detail(plan_no))


@router.put(
    "/{plan_no}",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="修改方案基础信息",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m11:plan:update"))],
)
async def update_plan(
    plan_no: str,
    body: PlanUpdateIn,
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: PlanService = Depends(get_plan_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """仅草稿或已驳回状态可修改；更换主刀时重新校验授权。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.update_plan(plan_no, body, operator=user))


@router.get(
    "/eligible-types",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="可选术式推荐",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m11:plan:view"))],
)
async def get_eligible_types(
    patient_id: int = Query(..., gt=0),
    preop_exam_no: Optional[str] = Query(None, max_length=32),
    svc: PlanService = Depends(get_plan_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """按患者条件推荐可用术式，并给出各术式的预估切削/RSB/组织量。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.get_eligible_types(patient_id, preop_exam_no))


# ---------- 角膜切削计算（核心） ----------

@router.post(
    "/{plan_no}/ablation/calculate",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="执行切削计算（不落库）",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m11:plan:update"))],
)
async def calculate_ablation(
    plan_no: str,
    body: AblationCalcIn,
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: AblationService = Depends(get_ablation_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """按 Munnerlyn 等算法计算切削深度与 RSB，返回完整推导过程。

    RSB < 250μm 时返回错误码 1104021 并给出优化建议。
    """
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.calculate(plan_no, body, operator=user))


@router.post(
    "/{plan_no}/ablation",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="确认切削参数并落库",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m11:plan:update"))],
)
async def confirm_ablation(
    plan_no: str,
    body: dict,
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: AblationService = Depends(get_ablation_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """服务端重算后落库，并重新计算方案最小 RSB。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.confirm(plan_no, body, operator=user))


# ---------- 晶体度数计算（核心） ----------

@router.post(
    "/{plan_no}/iol-calculation/calculate",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="执行晶体度数计算",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m11:plan:update"))],
)
async def calculate_iol(
    plan_no: str,
    body: IolCalcIn,
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: IolService = Depends(get_iol_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """多公式并行计算晶体度数，校验 ACD/WTW/内皮等眼内手术前置条件。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.calculate(plan_no, body, operator=user))


@router.post(
    "/{plan_no}/iol-calculation/select",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="选择实际晶体度数",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m11:plan:update"))],
)
async def select_iol_power(
    plan_no: str,
    body: IolSelectIn,
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: IolService = Depends(get_iol_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """写入实际选择度数并计算偏差，供 M09 通过 P-01 核对。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.select_power(plan_no, body, operator=user))


# ---------- 审核与签署 ----------

@router.post(
    "/{plan_no}/submit",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="提交审核",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m11:plan:create"))],
)
async def submit_plan(
    plan_no: str,
    body: dict,
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: PlanService = Depends(get_plan_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """校验计算完成、授权有效、风险已确认，生成版本快照并进入审核流。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.submit(plan_no, body, operator=user))


@router.post(
    "/{plan_no}/audit",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="审核通过 / 驳回 / 终审",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m11:plan:audit"))],
)
async def audit_plan(
    plan_no: str,
    body: PlanAuditIn,
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: PlanService = Depends(get_plan_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """强制重做 M01-07 授权校验，自动生成 11 项检查表后判定。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.audit(plan_no, body, operator=user))


@router.post(
    "/{plan_no}/consent",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="知情同意签署",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m11:plan:sign"))],
)
async def sign_consent(
    plan_no: str,
    body: ConsentSignIn,
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: PlanService = Depends(get_plan_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """固化方案快照与风险清单，生成 PDF 与 SHA-256。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.sign_consent(plan_no, body, operator=user))


# ---------- 内部契约（M06 / M09 / M16-04 调用） ----------

internal_router = APIRouter(prefix="/internal/v1/m11", tags=["M11-04 内部契约"])


@internal_router.get(
    "/iol-calculation/{patient_id}",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="P-01 晶体理论度数（M09-05 消费）",
)
async def internal_iol_calculation(
    patient_id: int,
    svc: IolService = Depends(get_iol_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """P-01：按患者返回当前有效晶体计算的理论度数、选择度数与偏差。

    走 uk_m11_iol_calc_current 单行唯一索引。无记录时返回 found=false（HTTP 200）。
    """
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.get_current_by_patient(patient_id))


@internal_router.get(
    "/plans/{plan_no}",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="P-04 取手术方案（含切削参数）",
)
async def internal_get_plan(
    plan_no: str,
    svc: PlanService = Depends(get_plan_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """P-04：供 M06 排台前校验、M09 术中核对、M16-04 收费项推导。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.get_plan_for_internal(plan_no))


@internal_router.get(
    "/plans/by-patient/{patient_id}/latest",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="P-05 患者最近一次方案",
)
async def internal_get_latest_plan(
    patient_id: int,
    svc: PlanService = Depends(get_plan_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """P-05：返回患者最近一次有效方案，优先级 AUDITED > PENDING_AUDIT > DRAFT。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.get_latest_by_patient(patient_id))


@internal_router.post(
    "/plans/on-audited",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="P-06 方案审核结果回调",
)
async def internal_on_audited(
    body: dict,
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    svc: PlanService = Depends(get_plan_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """P-06：M06 排台前校验回调，返回 can_schedule。

    本接口只读校验，不修改方案状态（状态由审核流唯一维护）。
    """
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.on_audited(body, idem_key=idempotency_key))
```

### 6.5 Service 层核心逻辑示例

```python
# app/services/m11/ablation_service.py
"""M11-04 角膜切削计算服务（核心算法实现）"""

from datetime import datetime
from decimal import ROUND_HALF_UP, Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.exceptions import BizException
from app.core.redis import redis_client
from app.models.m11.plan import (
    M11Plan,
    M11PlanAblation,
    M11PlanAblationCalc,
    M11PlanPreopSnap,
)
from app.schemas.m11.plan import AblationCalcIn
from app.services.m11.plan_log_service import PlanLogService

# 计算常量（§3.4）
CORNEA_REFRACTIVE_INDEX = Decimal("1.376")
TWO_N_MINUS_ONE = Decimal("0.752")          # 2 × (1.376 − 1)
CYL_DENOMINATOR = Decimal("1.504")          # 2 × TWO_N_MINUS_ONE

# 红线与阈值（与附录 E 系统参数一致，且数据库 CHECK 兜底）
RSB_REDLINE_UM = Decimal("250.00")
RSB_WARN_UM = Decimal("300.00")
OZ_MIN_MM = Decimal("5.50")
OZ_MAX_MM = Decimal("8.00")
TZ_MAX_MM = Decimal("9.50")
TZ_MIN_WIDTH_MM = Decimal("0.80")
ABLATION_MAX_UM = Decimal("200.00")
ALGO_VERSION = "2.1.0"


class AblationService:
    """角膜切削参数计算与 RSB 判定服务。"""

    def __init__(self, db: AsyncSession) -> None:
        self.db = db
        self.log = PlanLogService(db)

    # ---------- 主计算入口 ----------

    async def calculate(
        self,
        plan_no: str,
        req: AblationCalcIn,
        operator: Any,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """执行切削计算，返回完整推导过程并留痕（不写最终参数）。

        RSB 低于 250μm 时抛出 1104021，并附带优化建议。
        """
        started = datetime.now()
        plan = await self._get_plan(plan_no, writable=True)
        if plan.is_intraocular:
            raise BizException("1104013", "ICL 为眼内手术，请使用晶体度数计算")

        snap = await self._get_snapshot(plan.id)

        # 1) 光学区与过渡区校验（§3.4.2）
        self._validate_oz_tz(req.oz_mm, req.tz_mm)

        # 2) 输入校验（§3.4.3 前置条件）
        cct = snap.od_cct if req.eye_side == "OD" else snap.os_cct
        thinnest = snap.od_thinnest if req.eye_side == "OD" else snap.os_thinnest
        if cct is None:
            raise BizException("1104022", "中央角膜厚度缺失，无法计算 RSB")
        if thinnest is not None and thinnest > cct:
            raise BizException("1104022", "最薄点厚度大于中央厚度，数据异常")

        flap_cap = self._resolve_flap_cap(plan.refractive_type, req)

        # 3) 计算切削深度与组织量（§3.4.1 / §3.4.4）
        sphere_um, cyl_um, max_um = self.compute_ablation_depth(
            pre_sphere=snap.od_sphere if req.eye_side == "OD" else snap.os_sphere,
            pre_cylinder=(snap.od_cylinder if req.eye_side == "OD"
                          else snap.os_cylinder),
            oz_mm=req.oz_mm,
            target_sphere=req.target_sphere,
            nomogram_adjust_d=req.nomogram_adjust_d,
        )
        tissue = self.compute_tissue_volume(
            pre_sphere=snap.od_sphere if req.eye_side == "OD" else snap.os_sphere,
            oz_mm=req.oz_mm,
        )

        # 4) 计算 RSB 与裕度（§3.4.3 / §3.4.8）
        rsb_source = min(cct, thinnest) if thinnest is not None else cct
        rsb, status, margin, grade = self.compute_rsb(rsb_source, flap_cap, max_um)

        # 5) 切削深度上限校验
        if max_um > ABLATION_MAX_UM:
            raise BizException(
                "1104024",
                f"最大切削深度 {max_um}μm 超出设备上限 {ABLATION_MAX_UM}μm",
            )

        # 6) RSB 红线校验（§3.4.3）
        if rsb < RSB_REDLINE_UM:
            suggestions = self._build_suggestions(
                pre_sphere=snap.od_sphere, pre_cylinder=snap.od_cylinder,
                cct=rsb_source, flap_cap=flap_cap, refractive_type=plan.refractive_type,
            )
            raise BizException(
                "1104021",
                f"剩余基质床厚度 {rsb}μm 低于红线 {RSB_REDLINE_UM}μm，禁止该方案",
                data={
                    "rsb_um": float(rsb),
                    "redline_um": float(RSB_REDLINE_UM),
                    "eye_side": req.eye_side,
                    "suggestions": suggestions,
                },
            )

        # 7) 生成留痕（calc_seq 递增，落 m11_plan_ablation_calc）
        calc_seq = await self._next_calc_seq(plan.id, req.eye_side)
        calc = M11PlanAblationCalc(
            plan_id=plan.id,
            plan_no=plan.plan_no,
            eye_side=req.eye_side,
            calc_seq=calc_seq,
            algo_code=req.algo_code,
            algo_version=ALGO_VERSION,
            refractive_type=plan.refractive_type,
            pre_sphere=snap.od_sphere if req.eye_side == "OD" else snap.os_sphere,
            pre_cylinder=(snap.od_cylinder if req.eye_side == "OD"
                          else snap.os_cylinder),
            pre_axis=snap.od_axis if req.eye_side == "OD" else snap.os_axis,
            target_sphere=req.target_sphere,
            oz_mm=req.oz_mm,
            tz_mm=req.tz_mm,
            cct_um=cct,
            thinnest_um=thinnest,
            flap_thickness_um=flap_cap,
            nomogram_adjust_d=req.nomogram_adjust_d,
            sphere_component_um=sphere_um,
            cylinder_component_um=cyl_um,
            ablation_max_um=max_um,
            ablation_center_um=max_um,
            ablation_total_um=tissue,
            rsb_um=rsb,
            rsb_status=status,
            feasible=True,
            warn_flags=self._build_warn_flags(req, snap),
            is_adopted=False,
            calc_duration_ms=int((datetime.now() - started).total_seconds() * 1000),
            calc_by=operator.id,
            calc_by_name=operator.name,
            calc_at=datetime.now(),
        )
        self.db.add(calc)
        await self.db.flush()

        await self.log.record(
            plan_id=plan.id,
            plan_no=plan.plan_no,
            entity_type="ABLATION_CALC",
            entity_id=calc.id,
            action="CALC_ABLATION" if calc_seq > 1 else "CALC_ABLATION",
            action_desc=f"{req.eye_side} 切削计算：OZ {req.oz_mm}mm，"
                        f"深度 {max_um}μm，RSB {rsb}μm（{status}）",
            operator=operator,
            duration_ms=calc.duration_ms if hasattr(calc, "duration_ms") else None,
        )
        await self.db.commit()

        return {
            "calc_no": calc.calc_no,
            "calc_seq": calc_seq,
            "eye_side": req.eye_side,
            "algo_code": req.algo_code,
            "algo_version": ALGO_VERSION,
            "inputs": {
                "pre_sphere": float(calc.pre_sphere),
                "pre_cylinder": float(calc.pre_cylinder),
                "pre_axis": calc.pre_axis,
                "oz_mm": float(req.oz_mm),
                "tz_mm": float(req.tz_mm),
                "flap_thickness_um": float(flap_cap),
                "cct_um": float(cct),
                "thinnest_um": float(thinnest) if thinnest is not None else None,
                "rsb_source_um": float(rsb_source),
            },
            "intermediate": {
                "sphere_component_um": float(sphere_um),
                "cylinder_component_um": float(cyl_um),
                "iteration_count": 1,
                "converged": True,
            },
            "outputs": {
                "ablation_max_um": float(max_um),
                "ablation_center_um": float(max_um),
                "ablation_total_um": float(tissue),
                "rsb_um": float(rsb),
                "rsb_status": status,
                "thickness_margin_um": float(margin),
                "margin_grade": grade,
            },
            "formula_display": self._formula_display(
                snap, req, sphere_um, cyl_um, max_um, tissue, rsb_source,
                flap_cap, rsb,
            ),
            "feasible": True,
            "infeasible_reasons": None,
            "warn_flags": calc.warn_flags,
            "suggestions": [],
            "duration_ms": calc.calc_duration_ms,
        }

    # ---------- §3.4.1 Munnerlyn 切削深度 ----------

    @staticmethod
    def compute_ablation_depth(
        pre_sphere: Decimal,
        pre_cylinder: Decimal,
        oz_mm: Decimal,
        target_sphere: Decimal = Decimal("0.00"),
        nomogram_adjust_d: Optional[Decimal] = None,
    ) -> Tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
        """按 Munnerlyn 公式计算球镜分量、柱镜分量与最大切削深度（μm）。

        公式：
            d_sphere = (S × r²) / (2(n−1))
            d_cyl    = (C × r²) / (2 × 2(n−1))
            d_max    = d_sphere + d_cyl
        其中 r = OZ/2，2(n−1) = 0.752，n = 1.376。
        """
        s = abs(pre_sphere - target_sphere)
        c = abs(pre_cylinder)
        if nomogram_adjust_d:
            s = s + abs(nomogram_adjust_d)

        radius = oz_mm / Decimal("2")
        radius_sq = radius * radius

        sphere_um = (s * radius_sq) / TWO_N_MINUS_ONE * Decimal("1000")
        cyl_um = (c * radius_sq) / CYL_DENOMINATOR * Decimal("1000")
        max_um = sphere_um + cyl_um

        def _q2(v: Decimal) -> Decimal:
            return v.quantize(Decimal("0.01"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)

        return _q2(sphere_um), _q2(cyl_um), _q2(max_um)

    # ---------- §3.4.4 组织切削量 ----------

    @staticmethod
    def compute_tissue_volume(pre_sphere: Decimal, oz_mm: Decimal) -> Decimal:
        """计算组织切削总量 V = (π × S × (OZ/2)⁴) / (4(n−1))，单位 μm·mm²。"""
        s = abs(pre_sphere)
        radius = oz_mm / Decimal("2")
        r4 = radius * radius * radius * radius
        pi = Decimal("3.14159265358979")
        v = (pi * s * r4) / (Decimal("4") * Decimal("0.376")) * Decimal("1000")
        return v.quantize(Decimal("0.01"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)

    # ---------- §3.4.3 RSB 与 §3.4.8 裕度分级 ----------

    @staticmethod
    def compute_rsb(
        base_thickness: Decimal,
        flap_or_cap: Decimal,
        ablation_max: Decimal,
    ) -> Tuple[Decimal, str, Decimal, str]:
        """计算 RSB、判定状态、厚度余量与裕度分级。

        返回 (rsb_um, rsb_status, margin_um, margin_grade)
        """
        rsb = base_thickness - flap_or_cap - ablation_max
        margin = rsb - RSB_REDLINE_UM

        # ★ 判定用未舍入的精确值，避免 249.996 舍入为 250.00 而误判通过
        if rsb < RSB_REDLINE_UM:
            status = "VIOLATION"
        elif rsb < RSB_WARN_UM:
            status = "WARN"
        else:
            status = "PASS"

        if margin < Decimal("0"):
            grade = "VIOLATION"
        elif margin < Decimal("20"):
            grade = "CRITICAL"
        elif margin < Decimal("50"):
            grade = "WARN"
        elif margin < Decimal("100"):
            grade = "CAUTION"
        else:
            grade = "NORMAL"

        def _q2(v: Decimal) -> Decimal:
            return v.quantize(Decimal("0.01"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)

        return _q2(rsb), status, _q2(margin), grade

    # ---------- §3.4.2 OZ / TZ 推荐 ----------

    @staticmethod
    def recommend_oz(
        pupil_scotopic: Optional[Decimal],
        gap_mm: Decimal = Decimal("0.50"),
    ) -> Tuple[Decimal, Decimal, List[str]]:
        """推荐 OZ 与 TZ。返回 (oz_mm, tz_mm, warn_flags)。"""
        warns: List[str] = []
        if pupil_scotopic is None:
            pupil_scotopic = Decimal("6.00")
            warns.append("PUPIL_NOT_MEASURED")

        oz_raw = pupil_scotopic + gap_mm
        oz = (oz_raw / Decimal("0.10")).quantize(
            Decimal("1"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP) * Decimal("0.10")
        oz = max(OZ_MIN_MM, min(OZ_MAX_MM, oz))
        if oz_raw > OZ_MAX_MM:
            warns.append("OZ_SMALLER_THAN_PUPIL")

        tz_raw = oz + TZ_MIN_WIDTH_MM * Decimal("2")
        tz = (tz_raw / Decimal("0.10")).quantize(
            Decimal("1"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP) * Decimal("0.10")
        if tz > TZ_MAX_MM:
            tz = TZ_MAX_MM
            warns.append("TZ_TRUNCATED")

        return oz, tz, warns

    # ---------- 私有工具 ----------

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_oz_tz(oz_mm: Decimal, tz_mm: Decimal) -> None:
        """校验光学区与过渡区参数（§3.4.2 边界条件）。"""
        if not (OZ_MIN_MM <= oz_mm <= OZ_MAX_MM):
            raise BizException(
                "1104023",
                f"光学区 {oz_mm}mm 超出 {OZ_MIN_MM}~{OZ_MAX_MM}mm 范围",
            )
        if tz_mm < oz_mm:
            raise BizException("1104023", "过渡区外径必须大于等于光学区")
        if tz_mm > TZ_MAX_MM:
            raise BizException(
                "1104023", f"过渡区外径 {tz_mm}mm 超出上限 {TZ_MAX_MM}mm")
        width = (tz_mm - oz_mm) / Decimal("2")
        if width < TZ_MIN_WIDTH_MM:
            raise BizException(
                "1104023",
                f"过渡区宽度 {width}mm 不足 {TZ_MIN_WIDTH_MM}mm",
            )

    @staticmethod
    def _resolve_flap_cap(refractive_type: str, req: AblationCalcIn) -> Decimal:
        """按术式解析瓣/帽厚度：SMILE 用帽，FS-LASIK 用瓣，表层手术为 0。"""
        if refractive_type == "SMILE":
            if req.flap_thickness_um is None:
                raise BizException("1104022", "SMILE 必须填写帽厚度")
            return req.flap_thickness_um
        if refractive_type == "FS_LASIK":
            if req.flap_thickness_um is None:
                raise BizException("1104022", "FS-LASIK 必须填写瓣厚度")
            return req.flap_thickness_um
        return Decimal("0.00")

    @staticmethod
    def _build_warn_flags(req: AblationCalcIn, snap: M11PlanPreopSnap) -> List[str]:
        """构建告警标记数组。"""
        flags: List[str] = []
        if snap.od_thinnest is None or snap.os_thinnest is None:
            flags.append("THINNEST_NOT_MEASURED")
        pupil = snap.od_pupil_scotopic
        if pupil is not None and req.oz_mm < pupil + Decimal("0.50"):
            flags.append("OZ_SMALLER_THAN_PUPIL")
        return flags

    @staticmethod
    def _build_suggestions(
        pre_sphere: Decimal,
        pre_cylinder: Decimal,
        cct: Decimal,
        flap_cap: Decimal,
        refractive_type: str,
    ) -> List[str]:
        """RSB 越线时给出可执行的优化建议（§3.4.3 算例场景 E）。"""
        suggestions: List[str] = []

        # 建议一：缩小 OZ 至 6.00mm
        sphere_um, cyl_um, max_um = AblationService.compute_ablation_depth(
            pre_sphere, pre_cylinder, Decimal("6.00"))
        rsb_small = cct - flap_cap - max_um
        if rsb_small >= Decimal("250.00"):
            suggestions.append(
                f"缩小光学区至 6.00mm，预估 RSB {rsb_small}μm")

        # 建议二：改用 TransPRK（无瓣无帽）
        rsb_trans = cct - Decimal("0") - max_um
        if rsb_trans >= Decimal("250.00"):
            suggestions.append(
                f"改用 TransPRK（无瓣），预估 RSB {rsb_trans}μm")

        # 建议三：改用 ICL
        suggestions.append("改用 ICL 眼内镜手术（不切削角膜）")
        return suggestions

    def _formula_display(
        self,
        snap: M11PlanPreopSnap,
        req: AblationCalcIn,
        sphere_um: Decimal,
        cyl_um: Decimal,
        max_um: Decimal,
        tissue: Decimal,
        rsb_source: Decimal,
        flap_cap: Decimal,
        rsb: Decimal,
    ) -> List[str]:
        """生成公式代入过程的展示文本（供页面展示，便于医师核对）。"""
        s = abs(snap.od_sphere if req.eye_side == "OD" else snap.os_sphere)
        c = abs(snap.od_cylinder if req.eye_side == "OD" else snap.os_cylinder)
        radius = req.oz_mm / Decimal("2")
        return [
            f"d_sphere = ({s} × {radius}²) / 0.752 = {sphere_um} μm",
            f"d_cyl = ({c} × {radius}²) / 1.504 = {cyl_um} μm",
            f"d_max = {sphere_um} + {cyl_um} = {max_um} μm",
            f"V = (π × {s} × {radius}⁴) / 1.504 = {tissue} μm·mm²",
            f"RSB = {rsb_source} − {flap_cap} − {max_um} = {rsb} μm",
        ]

    async def _get_plan(self, plan_no: str, writable: bool = False) -> M11Plan:
        """取方案并校验状态。"""
        plan = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M11Plan).where(
                M11Plan.plan_no == plan_no, M11Plan.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalar_one_or_none()
        if plan is None:
            raise BizException("1104001", "手术方案不存在或已取消")
        if plan.status == "EXPIRED":
            raise BizException("1104005", f"方案已过有效期（{plan.expire_at}），请重新开立")
        if writable and plan.status not in ("DRAFT", "REJECTED"):
            raise BizException(
                "1104002", f"方案已{plan.status}，不可修改；如需调整请开立新方案")
        return plan

    async def _get_snapshot(self, plan_id: int) -> M11PlanPreopSnap:
        """取术前基线快照。"""
        snap = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M11PlanPreopSnap).where(M11PlanPreopSnap.plan_id == plan_id)
        )).scalar_one_or_none()
        if snap is None:
            raise BizException("1104018", "术前基线快照缺失")
        return snap

    async def _next_calc_seq(self, plan_id: int, eye_side: str) -> int:
        """生成本方案本眼的下一个计算序号。"""
        from sqlalchemy import func
        current = (await self.db.execute(
            select(func.coalesce(func.max(M11PlanAblationCalc.calc_seq), 0)).where(
                M11PlanAblationCalc.plan_id == plan_id,
                M11PlanAblationCalc.eye_side == eye_side)
        )).scalar()
        return int(current) + 1

    async def confirm(self, plan_no: str, body: Dict[str, Any],
                      operator: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """确认切削参数：服务端重算后落库，并同步方案最小 RSB。"""
        plan = await self._get_plan(plan_no, writable=True)
        calc_id = body.get("calc_id")
        calc = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M11PlanAblationCalc).where(
                M11PlanAblationCalc.id == calc_id,
                M11PlanAblationCalc.plan_id == plan.id)
        )).scalar_one_or_none()
        if calc is None or not calc.feasible:
            raise BizException("1104024", "切削计算记录不存在或不可行")

        # ★ 服务端重算，不采信前端传值
        rsb, status, margin, grade = self.compute_rsb(
            calc.cct_um if calc.thinnest_um is None else min(calc.cct_um, calc.thinnest_um),
            calc.flap_thickness_um or Decimal("0"),
            calc.ablation_max_um,
        )
        if rsb < RSB_REDLINE_UM:
            raise BizException(
                "1104021", f"剩余基质床厚度 {rsb}μm 低于红线 {RSB_REDLINE_UM}μm，禁止该方案")

        # UPSERT 切削参数
        existing = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M11PlanAblation).where(
                M11PlanAblation.plan_id == plan.id,
                M11PlanAblation.eye_side == calc.eye_side)
        )).scalar_one_or_none()

        payload = {
            "plan_id": plan.id,
            "plan_no": plan.plan_no,
            "patient_id": plan.patient_id,
            "eye_side": calc.eye_side,
            "refractive_type": plan.refractive_type,
            "pre_sphere": calc.pre_sphere,
            "pre_cylinder": calc.pre_cylinder,
            "pre_axis": calc.pre_axis,
            "target_sphere": calc.target_sphere,
            "cct_um": calc.cct_um,
            "thinnest_um": calc.thinnest_um,
            "oz_mm": calc.oz_mm,
            "tz_mm": calc.tz_mm,
            "ablation_max_um": calc.ablation_max_um,
            "ablation_center_um": calc.ablation_center_um,
            "ablation_total_um": calc.ablation_total_um,
            "flap_thickness_um": (calc.flap_thickness_um
                                  if plan.refractive_type == "FS_LASIK" else None),
            "cap_thickness_um": (calc.flap_thickness_um
                                 if plan.refractive_type == "SMILE" else None),
            "rsb_um": rsb,
            "rsb_status": status,
            "rsb_redline_um": RSB_REDLINE_UM,
            "thickness_margin_um": margin,
            "rsb_source_um": (calc.cct_um if calc.thinnest_um is None
                              else min(calc.cct_um, calc.thinnest_um)),
            "algo_code": calc.algo_code,
            "algo_version": calc.algo_version,
            "feasible": True,
            "confirm_by": operator.id,
            "confirm_at": datetime.now(),
        }

        if existing is None:
            self.db.add(M11PlanAblation(**payload))
        else:
            for key, value in payload.items():
                setattr(existing, key, value)

        # 采纳标记（部分唯一索引保证每方案每眼仅一条 TRUE）
        calc.is_adopted = True
        calc.adopted_at = datetime.now()

        await self.db.flush()

        # 同步方案最小 RSB
        min_rsb = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M11PlanAblation.rsb_um).where(
                M11PlanAblation.plan_id == plan.id,
                M11PlanAblation.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalars().all()
        if min_rsb:
            plan.min_rsb_um = min(min_rsb)
            plan.rsb_redline_violated = plan.min_rsb_um < RSB_REDLINE_UM
            plan.ablation_feasible = not plan.rsb_redline_violated

        await self.log.record(
            plan_id=plan.id,
            plan_no=plan.plan_no,
            entity_type="ABLATION",
            entity_id=calc.id,
            action="CONFIRM_ABLATION",
            action_desc=f"{calc.eye_side} 切削参数确认：OZ {calc.oz_mm}mm，RSB {rsb}μm",
            operator=operator,
            snapshot=payload,
        )
        await self.db.commit()

        confirmed = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M11PlanAblation.eye_side).where(
                M11PlanAblation.plan_id == plan.id,
                M11PlanAblation.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalars().all()

        return {
            "id": calc.id,
            "eye_side": calc.eye_side,
            "rsb_um": float(rsb),
            "rsb_status": status,
            "feasible": True,
            "plan_min_rsb_um": float(plan.min_rsb_um),
            "both_eyes_confirmed": set(confirmed) >= {"OD", "OS"},
        }
```

**代码说明：**

| 类/方法 | 职责 | 关键点 |
|---|---|---|
| `AblationService.calculate` | 切削计算主入口 | 校验 OZ/TZ、厚度基准、瓣帽 → 计算深度与 RSB → **RSB < 250 抛 `"1104021"` 并给建议** → 留痕 |
| `compute_ablation_depth` | §3.4.1 Munnerlyn | `d = (S × r²) / 0.752`，柱镜用 `/1.504`；2 位小数 `ROUND_HALF_UP` |
| `compute_tissue_volume` | §3.4.4 组织量 | `V = (π × S × r⁴) / (4 × 0.376)`，单位 μm·mm² |
| `compute_rsb` | §3.4.3 RSB + §3.4.8 裕度 | **判定用未舍入精确值**；返回 4 元组（RSB/状态/余量/分级） |
| `recommend_oz` | §3.4.2 OZ/TZ 推荐 | `OZ = clamp(暗瞳 + 0.50, 5.50, 8.00)`；TZ = OZ + 2×0.80，超 9.50 截断 |
| `_build_suggestions` | RSB 越线优化建议 | 缩 OZ 至 6.00mm、改 TransPRK、改 ICL，三条可执行路径 |
| `confirm` | 确认落库 | **服务端重算不采信前端**；UPSERT + 采纳标记 + 同步 `min_rsb_um` |
| `_formula_display` | 公式代入过程 | 生成 `d_sphere = (4.25 × 3.25²) / 0.752 = 59.70 μm` 供页面展示 |

```python
# app/services/m11/iol_service.py
"""M11-04 晶体度数计算服务（P-01 契约实现）"""

from datetime import datetime
from decimal import ROUND_HALF_UP, Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.exceptions import BizException
from app.core.m11.iol_formula import IOLFormulaEngine
from app.core.redis import redis_client
from app.models.m11.plan import (
    M11IolCalc,
    M11IolCalcDetail,
    M11Plan,
    M11PlanPreopSnap,
)
from app.schemas.m11.plan import IolCalcIn, IolSelectIn
from app.services.m11.plan_log_service import PlanLogService

# 眼内手术前置条件（附录 E 系统参数）
ACD_MIN_MM = Decimal("2.80")
WTW_MIN_MM = Decimal("10.00")
WTW_MAX_MM = Decimal("13.50")
ENDOTHELIAL_MIN = 2000
AL_MIN_MM = Decimal("18.00")
AL_MAX_MM = Decimal("36.00")
DEFAULT_TOLERANCE_D = Decimal("0.25")
OUTLIER_DELTA_D = Decimal("0.75")


class IolService:
    """晶体度数计算、偏差核对与 P-01 契约服务。"""

    def __init__(self, db: AsyncSession) -> None:
        self.db = db
        self.log = PlanLogService(db)
        self.engine = IOLFormulaEngine()

    # ---------- 计算 ----------

    async def calculate(self, plan_no: str, req: IolCalcIn,
                        operator: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """多公式并行计算晶体度数并留痕。"""
        started = datetime.now()
        plan = await self._get_plan_writable(plan_no)
        if not plan.is_intraocular:
            raise BizException("1104014", "SMILE 为角膜激光手术，请使用切削参数计算")

        # ★ 公式选择理由强制 ≥10 字
        if not req.formula_reason or len(req.formula_reason.strip()) < 10:
            raise BizException("1104030", "公式选择理由必须不少于 10 字")
        if req.formula_code == "VENDOR_NOMOGRAM" and not req.vendor_nomogram_code:
            raise BizException("1104030", "厂商 nomogram 必须填写编码")

        snap = await self._get_snapshot(plan.id)
        m = self._eye_inputs(snap, req.eye_side)

        # 输入范围校验
        if not (AL_MIN_MM <= m["axial_length"] <= AL_MAX_MM):
            raise BizException(
                "1104031",
                f"眼轴 {m['axial_length']}mm 超出 {AL_MIN_MM}~{AL_MAX_MM}mm 范围")

        # ★ 眼内手术安全前置条件
        checks = self._safety_checks(m)
        failed = [c for c in checks if not c["passed"]]
        if failed:
            first = failed[0]
            code = "1104033" if first["item"] == "角膜直径" else "1104032"
            raise BizException(
                code,
                f"{first['item']} {first['value']} 不满足 {first['range']}",
            )

        # 主用公式 + 交叉验证公式并行计算
        formulas = [req.formula_code] + [f for f in req.ial_models
                                         if f != req.formula_code]
        results: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for idx, f_code in enumerate(formulas):
            try:
                r = self.engine.compute(
                    formula_code=f_code,
                    axial_length=m["axial_length"],
                    k1=m["k1"],
                    k2=m["k2"],
                    acd=m["acd"],
                    lens_thickness=m["lens_thickness"],
                    wtw=m["wtw"],
                    refraction=m["refraction"],
                    vendor_nomogram_code=req.vendor_nomogram_code,
                )
            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
                raise BizException(
                    "1104034", f"晶体公式 {f_code} 计算失败，请稍后重试") from exc
            r["is_primary"] = idx == 0
            results.append(r)

        primary_power = results[0]["lens_power_d"]
        for r in results:
            r["deviation_from_primary_d"] = (
                None if r["is_primary"]
                else (r["lens_power_d"] - primary_power).quantize(Decimal("0.01"))
            )
            r["is_outlier"] = (
                r["deviation_from_primary_d"] is not None
                and abs(r["deviation_from_primary_d"]) > OUTLIER_DELTA_D
            )

        powers = [r["lens_power_d"] for r in results]
        spread = (max(powers) - min(powers)).quantize(Decimal("0.01"))

        # 度数对齐 0.25D 步进
        theoretical = self._quantize_quarter(primary_power)

        # 晶体尺寸与拱高（厂商 nomogram）
        size, size_rule, vault = self._resolve_size(
            m["wtw"], m["acd"], req.iol_model or "")

        # 库存可及性（弱依赖，失败降级）
        available, material_snap = await self._query_material(
            req.iol_material_code, theoretical)

        # 写入：新记录 is_current=TRUE，原记录置 SUPERSEDED
        await self._supersede_current(plan.patient_id)

        calc = M11IolCalc(
            patient_id=plan.patient_id,
            patient_name=plan.patient_name,
            plan_id=plan.id,
            plan_no=plan.plan_no,
            refractive_type=plan.refractive_type,
            eye_side=req.eye_side,
            iol_model=req.iol_model,
            iol_material_code=req.iol_material_code,
            formula_code=req.formula_code,
            formula_reason=req.formula_reason,
            formula_selected_by=operator.id,
            vendor_nomogram_code=req.vendor_nomogram_code,
            axial_length_mm=m["axial_length"],
            k1=m["k1"],
            k2=m["k2"],
            km=((m["k1"] + m["k2"]) / 2).quantize(Decimal("0.01")),
            anterior_chamber_depth_mm=m["acd"],
            lens_thickness_mm=m["lens_thickness"],
            white_to_white_mm=m["wtw"],
            refraction_sphere=m["refraction"],
            astigmatism_target=req.astigmatism_target,
            theoretical_power_d=theoretical,
            theoretical_se_d=theoretical,
            iol_size_mm=size,
            iol_size_rule=size_rule,
            vault_estimate_um=vault,
            material_available=available,
            material_available_at=datetime.now() if available is not None else None,
            material_snap=material_snap,
            status="DRAFT",
            is_current=True,
            calc_by=operator.id,
            calc_by_name=operator.name,
            calc_at=datetime.now(),
            calc_duration_ms=int((datetime.now() - started).total_seconds() * 1000),
        )
        self.db.add(calc)
        await self.db.flush()

        for r in results:
            self.db.add(M11IolCalcDetail(
                iol_calc_id=calc.id,
                calc_no=calc.calc_no,
                patient_id=plan.patient_id,
                eye_side=req.eye_side if req.eye_side != "OU" else "OD",
                formula_code=r["formula_code"],
                formula_version=r.get("formula_version"),
                is_primary=r["is_primary"],
                axial_length_mm=m["axial_length"],
                k1=m["k1"],
                k2=m["k2"],
                anterior_chamber_depth_mm=m["acd"],
                lens_thickness_mm=m["lens_thickness"],
                white_to_white_mm=m["wtw"],
                lens_power_d=r["lens_power_d"],
                pred_refraction_d=r.get("pred_refraction_d"),
                effective_iol_position=r.get("elp"),
                is_outlier=r["is_outlier"],
                deviation_from_primary_d=r["deviation_from_primary_d"],
                warning_message=r.get("warning_message"),
            ))

        await self.log.record(
            plan_id=plan.id,
            plan_no=plan.plan_no,
            entity_type="IOL_CALC",
            entity_id=calc.id,
            action="CALC_IOL",
            action_desc=f"{req.eye_side} 晶体计算：{req.formula_code} "
                        f"理论 {theoretical}D，多公式极差 {spread}D",
            operator=operator,
        )
        await self.db.commit()
        await redis_client.delete(f"m11:plan:iol:{plan.patient_id}")

        return {
            "calc_no": calc.calc_no,
            "eye_side": req.eye_side,
            "formula_code": req.formula_code,
            "theoretical_power_d": float(theoretical),
            "pred_refraction_d": results[0].get("pred_refraction_d"),
            "details": [
                {
                    "formula_code": r["formula_code"],
                    "lens_power_d": float(r["lens_power_d"]),
                    "is_primary": r["is_primary"],
                }
                for r in results
            ],
            "consistency": {
                "max_power": float(max(powers)),
                "min_power": float(min(powers)),
                "spread": float(spread),
                "consistent": spread < Decimal("1.00"),
                "outlier_count": sum(1 for r in results if r["is_outlier"]),
            },
            "iol_size_mm": float(size) if size is not None else None,
            "iol_size_rule": size_rule,
            "vault_estimate_um": float(vault) if vault is not None else None,
            "material_available": available,
            "safety_checks": checks,
            "duration_ms": calc.calc_duration_ms,
        }

    # ---------- §3.4.6 偏差判定 ----------

    async def select_power(self, plan_no: str, req: IolSelectIn,
                           operator: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """写入实际选择的晶体度数并判定偏差（M09 核对依据）。"""
        plan = await self._get_plan_writable(plan_no)
        calc = await self._get_current(plan.patient_id)
        if calc is None:
            raise BizException("1104037", "该患者暂无当前有效的晶体度数计算")

        theoretical = Decimal(str(calc.theoretical_power_d))
        selected = Decimal(str(req.selected_power_d))
        if not (Decimal("-30.00") <= selected <= Decimal("40.00")):
            raise BizException(
                "1104031", f"选择度数 {selected}D 超出 -30.00~+40.00D 范围")

        deviation = (selected - theoretical).quantize(Decimal("0.01"))
        tolerance = Decimal(str(calc.deviation_tolerance_d or DEFAULT_TOLERANCE_D))

        if abs(deviation) <= tolerance:
            status = "WITHIN_TOLERANCE"
        elif req.deviation_reason and len(req.deviation_reason.strip()) >= 10:
            status = "JUSTIFIED"
        else:
            raise BizException(
                "1104035",
                f"偏差 {deviation}D 超阈值 {tolerance}D，必须填写说明（不少于 10 字）",
            )

        calc.selected_power_d = selected
        calc.selected_sphere_d = req.selected_sphere_d
        calc.selected_cylinder_d = req.selected_cylinder_d
        calc.selected_axis = req.selected_axis
        calc.deviation_d = deviation
        calc.deviation_status = status
        calc.deviation_reason = req.deviation_reason
        if req.iol_size_mm is not None:
            calc.iol_size_mm = req.iol_size_mm

        await self.log.record(
            plan_id=plan.id,
            plan_no=plan.plan_no,
            entity_type="IOL_CALC",
            entity_id=calc.id,
            action="SELECT_IOL_POWER",
            action_desc=f"选择晶体度数 {selected}D（理论 {theoretical}D，"
                        f"偏差 {deviation}D，{status}）",
            operator=operator,
        )
        await self.db.commit()
        # P-01 缓存失效（M09 会立刻重新拉取）
        await redis_client.delete(f"m11:plan:iol:{plan.patient_id}")

        return {
            "calc_no": calc.calc_no,
            "theoretical_power_d": float(theoretical),
            "selected_power_d": float(selected),
            "deviation_d": float(deviation),
            "deviation_status": status,
            "deviation_tolerance_d": float(tolerance),
        }

    # ---------- ★ P-01 契约实现 ----------

    async def get_current_by_patient(self, patient_id: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """P-01：按患者返回当前有效晶体计算（M09-05 术中核对）。

        走 uk_m11_iol_calc_current 唯一索引，单行查找。
        无记录时返回 found=false（HTTP 200），便于 M09 区分「无计算」与「服务不可用」。
        """
        cache_key = f"m11:plan:iol:{patient_id}"
        cached = await redis_client.get(cache_key)
        if cached:
            import json
            return json.loads(cached)

        calc = await self._get_current(patient_id)
        if calc is None:
            return {"found": False, "patient_id": patient_id}

        result = {
            "found": True,
            "calc_no": calc.calc_no,
            "patient_id": calc.patient_id,
            "refractive_type": calc.refractive_type,
            "eye_side": calc.eye_side,
            "formula_code": calc.formula_code,
            "axial_length_mm": float(calc.axial_length_mm),
            "anterior_chamber_depth_mm": (
                float(calc.anterior_chamber_depth_mm)
                if calc.anterior_chamber_depth_mm is not None else None),
            "white_to_white_mm": (
                float(calc.white_to_white_mm)
                if calc.white_to_white_mm is not None else None),
            "theoretical_power_d": float(calc.theoretical_power_d),
            "selected_power_d": (float(calc.selected_power_d)
                                 if calc.selected_power_d is not None else None),
            "deviation_d": (float(calc.deviation_d)
                            if calc.deviation_d is not None else None),
            "deviation_status": calc.deviation_status,
            "deviation_tolerance_d": float(calc.deviation_tolerance_d),
            "iol_model": calc.iol_model,
            "iol_size_mm": (float(calc.iol_size_mm)
                            if calc.iol_size_mm is not None else None),
            "status": calc.status,
            "confirmed_at": (calc.confirmed_at.isoformat()
                             if calc.confirmed_at else None),
            "plan_no": calc.plan_no,
        }
        import json
        await redis_client.setex(cache_key, 120, json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False))
        return result

    # ---------- 私有工具 ----------

    @staticmethod
    def _eye_inputs(snap: M11PlanPreopSnap, eye_side: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """按眼别提取生物测量输入。"""
        od = eye_side in ("OD", "OU")
        return {
            "axial_length": snap.od_axial_length if od else snap.os_axial_length,
            "k1": snap.od_k1 if od else snap.os_k1,
            "k2": snap.od_k2 if od else snap.os_k2,
            "acd": snap.od_acd if od else snap.os_acd,
            "lens_thickness": snap.od_lt if od else snap.os_lt,
            "wtw": snap.od_wtw if od else snap.os_wtw,
            "refraction": snap.od_sphere if od else snap.os_sphere,
            "endothelial": (snap.od_endothelial_count if od
                            else snap.os_endothelial_count),
        }

    @staticmethod
    def _safety_checks(m: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """眼内手术安全前置条件校验（§3.4.5 边界条件）。"""
        acd = m["acd"] or Decimal("0")
        wtw = m["wtw"] or Decimal("0")
        endo = m["endothelial"] or 0
        al = m["axial_length"]
        return [
            {"item": "前房深度", "value": float(acd), "range": "≥2.80",
             "passed": acd >= ACD_MIN_MM},
            {"item": "角膜直径", "value": float(wtw), "range": "10.00~13.50",
             "passed": WTW_MIN_MM <= wtw <= WTW_MAX_MM},
            {"item": "内皮细胞计数", "value": endo, "range": "≥2000",
             "passed": endo >= ENDOTHELIAL_MIN},
            {"item": "眼轴长度", "value": float(al), "range": "18.00~36.00",
             "passed": AL_MIN_MM <= al <= AL_MAX_MM},
        ]

    @staticmethod
    def _quantize_quarter(value: Decimal) -> Decimal:
        """把晶体度数对齐到 0.25D 步进。"""
        q = (Decimal(str(value)) / Decimal("0.25")).quantize(
            Decimal("1"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP) * Decimal("0.25")
        return q.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))

    @staticmethod
    def _resolve_size(wtw: Optional[Decimal], acd: Optional[Decimal],
                      model: str) -> tuple:
        """按厂商 nomogram 选择晶体尺寸并预估拱高。"""
        if wtw is None:
            return None, None, None
        if wtw < Decimal("11.00"):
            size = Decimal("12.10")
        elif wtw < Decimal("11.50"):
            size = Decimal("12.60")
        elif wtw < Decimal("12.00"):
            size = Decimal("13.20")
        elif wtw < Decimal("12.50"):
            size = Decimal("13.70")
        else:
            size = Decimal("14.20")
        rule = (f"WTW {wtw}mm 与 ACD {acd}mm，按厂商 nomogram 选择 {size}mm 规格")
        vault = Decimal("520.00") if acd and acd >= Decimal("3.00") else Decimal("430.00")
        return size, rule, vault

    async def _query_material(self, material_code: Optional[str],
                              power: Decimal) -> tuple:
        """查 M15 库存可及性（弱依赖，失败降级为 None）。"""
        if not material_code:
            return None, None
        try:
            from app.clients.m11.m15_stock_client import M15StockClient
            resp = await M15StockClient().query_available(material_code, power)
            return bool(resp.get("available")), resp
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
            return None, None

    async def _supersede_current(self, patient_id: int) -> None:
        """把该患者原有的 current 记录置为 SUPERSEDED。"""
        existing = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M11IolCalc).where(
                M11IolCalc.patient_id == patient_id,
                M11IolCalc.is_current.is_(True),
                M11IolCalc.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalars().all()
        for rec in existing:
            rec.is_current = False
            rec.status = "SUPERSEDED"

    async def _get_current(self, patient_id: int) -> Optional[M11IolCalc]:
        """取患者当前有效晶体计算（走唯一索引）。"""
        return (await self.db.execute(
            select(M11IolCalc).where(
                M11IolCalc.patient_id == patient_id,
                M11IolCalc.is_current.is_(True),
                M11IolCalc.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalar_one_or_none()

    async def _get_plan_writable(self, plan_no: str) -> M11Plan:
        """取方案并校验可写状态。"""
        plan = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M11Plan).where(
                M11Plan.plan_no == plan_no, M11Plan.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalar_one_or_none()
        if plan is None:
            raise BizException("1104001", "手术方案不存在或已取消")
        if plan.status not in ("DRAFT", "REJECTED"):
            raise BizException(
                "1104002", f"方案已{plan.status}，不可修改；如需调整请开立新方案")
        return plan

    async def _get_snapshot(self, plan_id: int) -> M11PlanPreopSnap:
        """取术前基线快照。"""
        snap = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M11PlanPreopSnap).where(M11PlanPreopSnap.plan_id == plan_id)
        )).scalar_one_or_none()
        if snap is None:
            raise BizException("1104018", "术前基线快照缺失")
        return snap
```

**代码说明：**

| 类/方法 | 职责 | 关键点 |
|---|---|---|
| `IolService.calculate` | 晶体计算主入口 | 公式理由 ≥10 字校验、眼内安全前置条件、多公式并行、极差与离群判定 |
